Method: 引入Aether-7B-5Attn（6.59B参数的专家混合模型，约2.98B激活参数），其49层包含7种序列混合机制，按7×7拉丁方排列，保证每种机制在每行每列各出现一次，从而平衡深度暴露并消除放置混淆因素。构建参数匹配的代理模型（700.9M参数，4种机制，4×4拉丁方，16层，每臂8个随机种子）进行验证，并在2.16倍规模（1.514B参数）上复现。

Result: 将分布式异构堆叠重排为平衡周期循环，验证损失仅变化0.16%，说明精确放置影响很小；将相同机制聚成连续深度带产生0.59%惩罚；用同构堆叠替换异构堆叠产生1.68%惩罚。在2.16倍规模下，同构堆叠惩罚增至2.63%，移除SSM家族机制导致3.20%退化。

Conclusion: 性能主要取决于跨深度分布的异构组合，而非任何特定排列。论文还报告了各机制成本画像、英语和韩语评估及49层的因果安全审计，并公开模型权重、训练配方、代码、日志和架构源码。

Abstract: Since GPT, most Transformers have repeated the same attention mechanism at every layer. Yet this design is largely a convention rather than a tested conclusion. When multiple sequence mixers are combined in one stack, improvements may arise from mechanism choice, placement, or both, making causal attribution difficult. We introduce Aether-7B-5Attn, a 6.59B-parameter mixture-of-experts model ($\approx$2.98B active) whose 49 layers contain seven sequence-mixing mechanisms arranged as a $7\times7$ Latin square. Because each mechanism appears exactly once in every row and column, the design guarantees balanced exposure across depth while eliminating placement confounds. To evaluate this principle, we build a parameter-matched proxy with four mechanisms arranged as a $4\times4$ Latin square over sixteen layers, matched to 700.9M parameters and trained with eight seeds per arm. The results reveal a clear dissociation. Rearranging a distributed heterogeneous stack into a balanced periodic cycle changes validation loss by only 0.16\%, indicating that exact placement has little effect. In contrast, clustering the same mechanisms into contiguous depth bands incurs a 0.59\% penalty, while replacing the heterogeneous stack with a homogeneous one incurs a 1.68\% penalty. These results indicate that performance depends primarily on heterogeneous composition distributed across depth rather than on any particular permutation. We confirm this finding at 2.16$\times$ larger scale (1.514B parameters), where the homogeneous-stack penalty increases to 2.63\% and removing the SSM-family mechanism produces a 3.20\% degradation. We further report per-mechanism cost profiles, English and Korean evaluations, and a causal-safety audit of all 49 layers. We release model weights, training recipes, training code, logs, and architecture source code.

</details>


### [248] [Labeled Incidence Structures for Native Transformer Modeling of Text, Knowledge Graphs, and Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20278)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标签关联结构（LIS），用统一的 (x_d, s, e) 表示编码文本、知识图谱与超图中的端点，使单一标准 Transformer 能原生处理多种结构数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将文本、知识图谱、超图数据展平为 token 序列，会丢失各元素在关系实例中扮演不同角色的结构性信息；同时知识库中与存储位置绑定的标识符会使模型对存储顺序敏感，而自由学习的标识符随规模增大难以控制。

Method: 定义 LIS 表示，每个端点编码为内容 x_d、角色/槽位 s 与关系实例 e；通过槽位算子与实例算子组合构造结构地址 A(s,e)=R_s R_e，并刻画其唯一且路径无关的条件；据此定义相对传输 P_{j→i}=A_i^{-1}A_j，为注意力提供角色与关系感知的归纳偏置，无需人为序列顺序；同时对"位置项+关系项"的加性编码进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了加性编码无法捕获位置与关系联合作用的信息（仅能刻画二者各自效应）；指出由内容计算关系实例算子可避免存储顺序问题，在固定架构与 Lipschitz 假设下给出与仓库规模 M 无关的容量界。

Conclusion: LIS 提供了一种不依赖展平的统一结构表示，将结构差异交由算子而非架构承载，为多类型结构化数据的原生建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Text, knowledge graphs, and hypergraphs all have elements that play distinct roles within relation instances, structure that is lost when data is flattened into token sequences. We introduce labeled incidence structures (LIS), a uniform representation that encodes each endpoint as $(x_d, s, e)$: content $x_d$, a role or slot $s$, and the relation instance $e$ in which that role appears. Because every data type maps to the same $(x_d, s, e)$ representation without flattening, a single standard transformer can process them all natively, structural differences are carried entirely by the operators, not the architecture.
  LIS assigns a structural address to each endpoint by composing a slot operator and an instance operator, $A(s,e) = R_s R_e$. We characterize when this factorization gives every token a unique, path-independent address. When it does, the natural operator comparing endpoint $j$ to endpoint $i$ is the relative transport $P_{j\to i} = A_i^{-1} A_j$, which gives attention a role- and relation-aware inductive bias without imposing an arbitrary sequence order.
  Additive encodings of the form "position term plus relation term" can miss information that depends jointly on $s$ and $e$. We prove this in a controlled example family: when the journey operator is approximated by the sum of a position-only term and a relation-only term, the approximation cannot capture how position and relation combine, only their separate effects.
  We also analyze persistent knowledge repositories. Identifiers tied to storage locations make models sensitive to storage order, while freely learned identifiers can become harder to control as the repository size $M$ grows relative to the sample size $n$. Computing relation-instance operators from content avoids this storage-order issue and yields a capacity bound independent of $M$, under fixed architectural and Lipschitz assumptions.

</details>


### [249] [Personalising a Cross-User Surface Electromyography Encoder Under a Small Calibration Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20296)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究肌电接口校准中如何利用少量带标注重复样本进行个性化，比较了原型自适应、线性探针、缩放微调和全量微调四种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将校准视为一个数量问题，但未探讨设备在收集到校准重复样本后应如何处理它们。本文把跨用户编码器的个性化视为一项有成本的设计决策。

Method: 在单一跨用户编码器基础上，对每个留出受试者测试四种使用相同标注重复样本的方案：原型自适应、线性探针、缩放微调和全量微调，并在各数据库允许的最大预算内逐一测试（DB1五次、DB2和DB5四次重复），覆盖77名受试者。

Result: 在全量微调在每个预算下都最准确，且在受试者子集中无例外。但无梯度的原型规则在无优化器、无每用户权重副本的情况下，可恢复其增益的52%至78%，使个性化可在佩戴时完成。认为好表征只需新分类器的普遍直觉在此不成立。各方法相对于诊所拟合的每用户分类器的表现取决于具体数据库。

Conclusion: 全量微调精度最高，但原型自适应在工程上更具实用性，可在佩戴时无需优化器和额外权重副本完成个性化；个性化方法的效果依赖于具体数据库。

Abstract: A myoelectric interface needs calibration from the user before it will function. Earlier work has treated calibration as a quantity, but has not asked the question of what a device should do with the calibration repetitions once they have been collected. This paper views personalizing the cross-user encoder as a design decision with a cost. Four alternative approaches to using exactly the same labeled repetitions were tested from a single cross-user encoder per held-out subject. Prototypical adaptation, linear probes, scaled fine-tuning and full fine-tuning were tested at every budget up to the maximum each database allows, five repetitions on DB1 and four on DB2 and DB5. Comparing four ways to spend a small calibration budget across 77 subjects, full fine-tuning is the most accurate at every budget, consistently enough that there is no exception among subsets of subjects. The result which impacts how one might make an engineering decision however is that a gradient free prototypical rule recovers 52 to 78 per cent of its benefit with no optimiser and no per-user copy of the weights, which makes personalisation something a worn device can do at donning time. The widespread intuition that a good representation only needs a fresh classifier is incorrect here. How well each method may perform relative to a per-user classifier that would be fitted by a clinic will depend on the specific database.

</details>


### [250] [Intact-to-Amputee Transfer in Surface-EMG Gesture Decoding: Training Source and Calibration Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20297)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统综述了1077项研究（截肢者仅占约六分之一），并在11名经桡截肢者上验证了一个与电极布局无关的跨用户肌电编码器：零样本跨人群迁移完全失败，但加入少量标注重复后性能显著优于每位用户的临床定制分类器；此外，用健侧受试者训练比用其他截肢者训练迁移效果更好，两者结合更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌电识别器通常难以在不同个体间迁移，实际使用往往需要终端用户重新提供标注数据做校准；同时文献中截肢者样本严重不足（1077项研究中仅约六分之一涉及截肢者），因此需要检验跨用户编码器在截肢人群中的真实迁移边界。

Method: 采用系统综述统计1077项研究的人群分布；将一套与蒙太奇（电极布局）无关的跨用户编码器，在与健侧肢体训练数据相匹配的协议下迁移到11名经桡截肢者，比较零样本迁移、少样本标注校准、以及不同来源训练池（40名健侧受试者 vs 10名其他截肢者 vs 二者结合）的迁移效果，并检验预先注册的假设。

Result: 零样本跨人群迁移完全失败；加入标注数据后，3次重复时宏F1高出临床定制分类器0.190，且队列中每位受试者均如此；3次标注重复下达0.779宏F1，而逐用户流程为0.589；用40名健侧受试者训练优于用10名截肢者训练，两者结合效果最佳。预先注册的预测仅在具备若干次重复且源池加入更多截肢者后成立，在单次重复及与健侧对比匹配的各类预算下均不成立。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了"基线强度"解释的适用边界：跨用户肌电编码器无法实现零样本跨人群迁移，必须依赖新用户的少量标注数据；且源数据中截肢者与健侧受试者结合能带来最佳迁移效果，说明需要针对截肢人群补充数据的必要性。

Abstract: A recogniser trained on one person rarely transfers to the next, and useful performance usually demands a fresh round of labelled calibration from the end user. A systematic review of 1077 studies quantifies where the evidence is thin: amputees appear in about one in six. Here a montage-agnostic cross-user encoder is carried to eleven transradial amputees on a protocol matched to its intact-limb training data. Zero-shot cross-population transfer fails outright: the encoder requires labeled data from the new user before it begins decoding, and it then exceeds the per-user classifier a clinic would fit by 0.190 macro F1 at three repetitions and for every subject in the cohort. Given three labelled repetitions it reaches 0.779 macro-F1 against 0.589 for the per-user pipeline. Training on forty intact subjects produces better transfers to a new amputee than training on ten other amputees, and combining the two produces better transfers than either individually. The prediction pre-registered for this study, which extends the encoder's baseline-strength account with the premise that amputee EMG is less separable, holds true only after a few repetitions become available and after enriching the source pool with additional amputees. At a single repetition, and at every budget under a source matched to the intact-limb comparison, it fails. Thus, it locates the boundary of the proposed account.

</details>


### [251] [A Learning Algorithm for Threshold Boolean Networks with Prescribed Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20298)
*Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可微分损失函数学习算法，用于推断具有指定不动点的阈值布尔网络（TBN），在拟南芥FOS-GRN模型上实现完美重建并抑制伪吸引子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈值布尔网络（TBN）常用于建模基因调控等生物网络，其推断通常需满足特定的动力学约束（如指定不动点）。传统方法（如感知机、逻辑回归）虽能恢复不动点，但易引入大量伪吸引子，破坏网络动力学的真实性。因此需要一种能同时保证不动点保留并抑制伪吸引子的学习方法。

Method: 提出一种学习算法，采用自定义可微分损失函数，联合实现：1）保留指定不动点；2）惩罚伪吸引子；3）鼓励二值输出；4）通过L1正则化促进稀疏性。并分析了稀疏系数λ对性能与网络结构特性的影响。

Result: 在拟南芥FOS-GRN模型上，30次独立运行中有5次实现完美重建（全部10个目标不动点且无伪吸引子），平均恢复8.53±0.90个正确不动点且无伪吸引子。相比之下，感知机和逻辑回归最多恢复10个不动点，但引入了8至31个伪吸引子。稀疏系数λ（至0.01）变化时，性能与推断网络的结构特性保持稳健。

Conclusion: 结果表明该算法在指定动力学约束下能有效且稳定地捕捉有意义的网络动力学，优于标准方法，具有良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present a learning algorithm for inferring threshold Boolean networks (TBNs) with a prescribed set of fixed points. The proposed method employs a custom differentiable loss function that jointly enforces fixed point preservation, penalizes spurious attractors, encourages binary outputs, and promotes sparsity through L1 regularization. Applied to the FOS-GRN model of Arabidopsis thaliana, the approach achieved perfect reconstruction (i.e., all 10 desired fixed points and no spurious ones) in 5 out of 30 independent runs, recovering on average 8.53 $\pm$ 0.90 correct fixed points with no spurious attractors. In contrast, standard methods such as the Perceptron and Logistic Regression recovered up to 10 fixed points but introduced between 8 and 31 spurious ones. An additional analysis varying the sparsity coefficient ($λ$) confirmed that the method's performance and the structural properties of the inferred networks remain robust within a practical range (up to 0.01) of regularization strengths. Overall, the results demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed algorithm in capturing meaningful network dynamics under prescribed dynamical constraints.

</details>


### [252] [Improving Generalization and Robustness in Offline Reinforcement Learning via Boundary-Aware Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20300)
*Gong Gao,Weidong Zhao,Xianhui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边界感知数据增强方法 BADA，用于提升离线强化学习的分布内泛化性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习算法易过拟合训练数据，部署到真实环境时分布内泛化与鲁棒性较差。已有方法多借用计算机视觉中的正则化技术，但由于低层物理信号对分布偏移高度敏感，这些方法仍存在明显局限，难以在复杂环境中保持稳定性能。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了采用随机片段插值训练的行为策略与动作价值函数的误差界，发现误差与状态间距离正相关。基于此提出 BADA，利用邻近状态构建插值边界，生成更忠实保留原始数据分布的合成数据。

Result: 在玩具环境中的定性研究表明，BADA 生成的混合样本在保持策略平滑性的同时能准确重建多模态价值分布；在多个受限离线数据集基准上的大量实验显示 BADA 达到了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析与边界感知插值数据增强，BADA 有效缓解了离线强化学习中的过拟合问题，提升了分布内泛化与鲁棒性，并在多项基准上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Current offline reinforcement learning (ORL) algorithms tend to overfit the training dataset and exhibit poor in-distribution generalization and robustness performance when deployed to real environments, thus compromising their effectiveness. Existing methods typically enhance in-distribution generalization and robustness by leveraging regularization techniques widely used in computer vision. However, due to the high sensitivity of low-level physical signals to distributional shifts, these methods still suffer from notable limitations in in-distribution generalization and robustness, making it difficult to achieve stable performance in complex environments. To address this issue, we theoretically analyze the error bounds of the behavior policy and action-value function trained with random episode interpolation, revealing that the error scales positively correlated with the distance between states. Based on this insight, we propose a method called $\bf{B}$oundary-$\bf{A}$ware $\bf{D}$ata $\bf{A}$ugmentation (BADA), which leverages neighboring states to construct interpolation boundaries, enabling the generation of synthetic data that more faithfully preserves the original data distribution. We first conduct qualitative studies in a toy environment, showing that BADA generates mixed samples that preserve desirable policy smoothness while accurately reconstructing multimodal value distributions. Extensive experiments on limited offline datasets further demonstrate that BADA attains state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [253] [SAGG: Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating for Robust Multimodal Learning under Heterogeneous Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20302)
*Wentao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Yutong Zhang,Wentao Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态训练中样本级异构损坏问题，提出样本自适应梯度门控方法 SAGG，实现无偏梯度估计并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态梯度平衡方法通常为每个模态使用共享标量调节梯度，隐含假设训练批次内损坏是均匀的。但实际中损坏具有样本异构性，同一小批次内不同样本可能损坏不同模态，导致批次级、样本无关的估计器产生不可消除的偏差。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明，在异构损坏模型下，任何批次级、样本无关且共享调制参数的线性估计器都会相对干净数据梯度产生不可消除的偏差；而样本级全有或全无门控在自然的无分布估计器类中是唯一无偏策略。基于此提出 Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating (SAGG)，通过在线特征范数质量检验对每个样本做二元保留或丢弃决策，并引入截断机制控制方差。同时给出 SAGG-SGD 的收敛性证明，并推导独立编码器架构下的认证鲁棒半径。

Result: 理论方面，SAGG 驱动的 SGD 能以标准 O(1/sqrt(T)) 速率收敛到干净损失的稳定点，且无依赖损坏的误差下限；同时建立了连接各模态 Lipschitz 常数与分类间隔的认证鲁棒半径。实验方面，在 Kinetics-Sounds 和 UCF-101 上，针对高斯噪声注入、部分模态缺失和自然贡献不平衡等设置，SAGG 持续优于十种现有方法，在高损坏场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论和实验两方面表明，样本级自适应门控是应对样本异构损坏的有效策略，SAGG 能在无偏估计、收敛保证和鲁棒性上同时取得优势，尤其适用于批次级偏差最严重的高损坏场景。

Abstract: Multimodal gradient balancing methods modulate encoder gradients with a shared scalar per modality, implicitly assuming that corruption is uniform across the training batch. In practice, corruption is sample-heterogeneous: within a single mini-batch, different samples may have different modalities corrupted. We prove that under this heterogeneous corruption model, any batch-level sample-agnostic linear estimator with a shared modulation parameter incurs an irreducible bias with respect to the clean-data gradient, and that sample-level all-or-nothing gating is the unique unbiased strategy within a natural distribution-free estimator class. Motivated by this result, we propose Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating (SAGG), which makes a binary retain-or-discard decision per sample via an online feature-norm quality test and incorporates a truncation mechanism for variance control. We prove that SAGG-based SGD converges at the standard O(1/sqrt(T)) rate to stationary points of the clean loss without a corruption-dependent error floor, and derive a certified robustness radius for the independent-encoder architecture that connects per-modality Lipschitz constants to the classification margin. Experiments on Kinetics-Sounds and UCF-101 under Gaussian noise injection, partial modality missing, and natural contribution imbalance show that SAGG consistently outperforms ten existing methods, with the largest gains in high-corruption regimes where batch-level bias is most severe.

</details>


### [254] [Hypernetwork-Parameterized Spatially Adaptive Neural Operators for PDE Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20309)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Chaoning Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Meng Ye,Xiaofeng Zhang,Qiang He,Weifeng Huang,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang,Caiyan Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出空间自适应神经算子SANO，用坐标条件超网络生成空间连续的算子参数场，替代空间共享参数化，在多维PDE基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间异质PDE具有位置相关的动力学特性，源于几何与物理系数的变化。现有神经算子虽通过多尺度特征、注意力或区域分解改进局部建模，但其更新规则往往仍是空间共享的；基于超网络的方法虽能跨PDE实例自适应参数，但通常每个实例只生成一个全局参数化。因此共享算子在边界与高梯度区域易欠拟合，且这些局部误差在自回归推理中会累积。

Method: 提出空间自适应神经算子（SANO），用位置相关的算子参数所构成的空间连续场替代空间共享参数化。SANO使用傅里叶编码坐标与坐标条件超网络，在采样点处生成空间算子条件编码；并提出超神经单元（HNE）机制，在局部子区域内插值这些编码，耦合邻近算子同时允许其更新规则随空间变化；最后用单位分解（partition-of-unity）权重组装重叠的局部预测。

Result: 在一维、二维、三维PDE以及两个带孔域椭圆基准上，SANO持续优于有竞争力的神经算子、超网络类与物理信息类基线。

Conclusion: 通过将算子参数化从空间共享转变为空间连续的位置相关场，SANO能更有效地建模空间异质PDE中的边界与高梯度区域，缓解自回归误差累积，实验证明其性能优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Spatially heterogeneous partial differential equations (PDEs) exhibit location-dependent dynamics arising from variations in geometry and physical coefficients. Existing neural operators improve localized modeling through multiscale features, attention mechanisms, or domain decomposition, yet their update rules often remain spatially shared. Hypernetwork-based methods adapt parameters across PDE instances but typically generate only one global parameterization per instance. Consequently, shared operators may underfit boundaries and high-gradient regions, with these localized errors accumulating during autoregressive rollout. We propose a spatially adaptive neural operator (SANO), which replaces this spatially shared parameterization with a spatially continuous field of location-dependent operator parameters. SANO uses Fourier-encoded coordinates and a coordinate-conditioned hypernetwork to generate spatial operator-conditioning codes at sampling points. A Hyper-Neural Element (HNE) mechanism interpolates these codes within local subregions, coupling neighboring operators while allowing their update rules to vary across space, and partition-of-unity weights assemble the overlapping local predictions. Experiments on one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDEs and two perforated-domain elliptic benchmarks show that SANO consistently outperforms competitive neural-operator, hypernetwork-based, and physics-informed baselines.

</details>


### [255] [ZeroHAT: Behavior-Conditioned Zero-Shot Human Activity Trace Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20310)
*Rongchao Xu,Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeroHAT：通过跨区域行为模式迁移与上下文适配，零样本生成目标区域的人类活动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类活动轨迹（HAT）对移动性预测和城市模拟等应用至关重要，但大规模采集成本高且涉及隐私问题。合成数据是可行替代方案，然而现有方法多依赖目标区域真实数据来生成同区域数据，对于缺乏真实HAT的区域不可行，因此需要零样本跨区域生成方法。

Method: 提出ZeroHAT，一个行为条件化的零样本HAT生成框架：从源区域真实HAT中学习行为模式，并结合目标区域公开上下文信息进行适配。包含三个核心组件：（i）多维一致性感知意图提取器；（ii）跨区域行为克隆模块；（iii）行为条件化的活动实现模块。

Result: 在十城市基准上评估，ZeroHAT的下游效用归一化指标达到最强基线的4.5–6.4倍，平均保真度提升15.6%–40.8%。

Conclusion: ZeroHAT证明了在无目标区域真实数据条件下，通过跨区域行为迁移实现高质量HAT合成的可行性，为数据稀缺区域提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Human activity traces record individuals' timestamped visits to points of interest and are essential for applications such as mobility prediction and urban simulation. However, accessing large-scale HATs is challenging due to high collection costs and privacy concerns. Synthetic HAT generation offers a promising way to make such data available and has attracted growing interest from both industry and academia. Although many efforts have been devoted to this topic, most of them rely on real data from a region to generate synthetic data for the same region, which is infeasible for the many regions where real HATs are unavailable. To fill this gap, we propose ZeroHAT, a behavior-conditioned framework that generates synthetic HATs for a target region in a zero-shot manner by transferring behavioral patterns learned from real HATs in source regions and adapting them with publicly available contextual information about the target region. ZeroHAT has three key novel components: (i) a multidimensional consistency-aware intent extractor; (ii) a cross-region behavioral cloning module; and (iii) a behavior-conditioned activity realization module. We evaluate ZeroHAT on a ten-city benchmark, where extensive experiments show that ZeroHAT achieves 4.5-6.4x the normalized downstream utility of the strongest baseline and improves average fidelity by 15.6%-40.8% across target regions.

</details>


### [256] [EviRec: Continual Evidence Learning for Dual Cold-Start POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20313)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EviRec，一个面向持续双冷启动 POI 推荐的证据学习框架，通过候选级可靠性门控融合匹配、转移记忆与生命周期三视角证据，在真实城市签到数据上显著优于现有方法，冷启动提升尤为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 POI 推荐方法大多假设用户群体与 POI 目录固定不变。作者通过对美国 10 个城市的大规模数据分析，发现存在显著的 POI 更替、用户流失、类别漂移以及静态 POI 记忆衰减，由此提出"持续双冷启动 POI 推荐"这一新问题设定。

Method: EviRec 是一个持续证据学习框架，对每个候选 POI 分别估计历史证据的可信度。它从三个互补视角为可见候选打分：基于用户近期移动画像的匹配视角、捕捉重复移动习惯的转移记忆视角、以及反映候选成熟度的生命周期视角。由于接近零的转移分数既可能表示不相关，也可能表示观测不足，EviRec 利用每个候选的观测状态对证据进行限定，并通过可靠性门控在转移记忆证据与生命周期证据之间自适应路由。

Result: 在覆盖全年、五个城市、包含 3 万余名用户与 684,200 条轨迹的 POI 签到数据集上进行评估。EviRec 持续优于当前最先进基线，且提升集中于冷启动查询：在 Dual-New 场景下，NDCG@10 相比最强基线提升 20.4%。

Conclusion: 深入分析表明，性能提升主要来自候选级可靠性门控，同时在很大程度上保留了此前学到的移动习惯模式，验证了该框架在持续双冷启动场景下的有效性与稳定性。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation is a core task in location-based services, yet most existing methods assume a fixed user population and POI catalog. Through a large-scale data-driven analysis of 10 U.S. cities, we identify substantial POI churn, user turnover, category drift, and decay in static POI memory, motivating the study of continual dual cold-start POI recommendation. To address this setting, we propose EviRec, a continual evidence-learning framework that estimates how much historical evidence should be trusted separately for each candidate POI. EviRec scores each visible candidate from three complementary views: a matching view based on the user's recent mobility profile, a transition-memory view that captures repeated mobility routines, and a lifecycle view that reflects candidate maturity. Because a near-zero transition score may indicate either irrelevance or insufficient observation, EviRec qualifies the evidence using each candidate's observation state and applies a reliability gate to adaptively route between transition-memory and lifecycle evidence. We evaluate EviRec on a full-year, five-city POI check-in dataset containing more than 30,000 users and 684,200 trajectories. Experimental results show that EviRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with the largest gains concentrated on cold-start queries. In particular, EviRec improves NDCG@10 by 20.4\% on Dual-New cases over the strongest baseline. In-depth analyses further confirm that these gains arise primarily from candidate-specific reliability gating while largely preserving previously learned mobility routines.

</details>


### [257] [Sharp Reconstruction Bounds for Autoencoders Using the Same Forward Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20333)
*Patricia Medina,Hy P. G. Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究自编码器中"置零-重构"机制的理论下界，并在地球LiDAR森林扫描数据上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注一种特定的自编码器结构：在将观测坐标置零前后施加相同的前向映射，并希望从理论上刻画其重构误差的下界以及哪些映射能达到该界。

Method: 在输入与隐藏维度相等且为奇数、维度d≥3的情形下，作者在雅可比奇异值落在[m,M]区间的保向微分同胚类中，推导最小一致重构导数误差；分析仿射映射是否可在任意给定深度达到该紧界；构造平移径向旋转说明精确重构特定球与奇异值接近1可同时成立；随后在798,452点的地面LiDAR森林扫描数据上进行实验，涉及四个空间区域、两个深度和三个随机种子。

Result: 理论上最小一致重构导数误差为max{1-M(M-m)/2,0}，仿射映射在每个规定深度都达到该紧界；存在平移径向旋转可精确重构任意给定球且奇异值任意接近1。实验上，在输入尺度0.05时，平均理论界为0.155，约为平均归一化训练误差0.185的84%；在该尺度下，增加一个隐藏坐标可将平均重构误差降至6×10⁻⁶以下。

Conclusion: 该工作为置零重构型自编码器给出了紧的重构误差界，并指出仅有界奇异值不足以保证有限数据下的重构质量，需要附加条件；实验结果与理论预测在数量级和趋势上基本一致。

Abstract: We study reconstruction in autoencoders that apply the same forward map before and after setting the observed coordinates to zero. For equal odd input and hidden dimensions $d\geq 3$, among orientation-preserving diffeomorphisms whose Jacobian singular values lie in $[m,M]$, we show that the least uniform reconstruction-derivative error is $\max\{1-M(M-m)/2,0\}$, with affine maps attaining this sharp bound at every prescribed depth. A translated radial rotation can nevertheless reconstruct any prescribed ball exactly with singular values arbitrarily close to one, motivating additional conditions for a finite-data bound. We test this prediction on a 798,452-point terrestrial LiDAR forest scan. At input scale $0.05$, the mean theoretical bound is $0.155$, about $84\%$ of the mean normalized training error $0.185$ across four spatial regions, two depths, and three seeds. At this scale, adding one hidden coordinate reduces the mean reconstruction error below $6\times10^{-6}$.

</details>


### [258] [COMPASS: Ordered Clustered Routing at 100K Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20352)
*Ido Greenberg,Hugo Linsenmaier,Piotr Sielski,Shie Mannor,Alex Fender,Gal Chechik,Eli Meirom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 COMPASS 算法求解有序聚类旅行商问题（OCTSP），按簇结构分解结合学习加速并行子求解器，可扩展至 10 万合成节点与 2.85 万真实电商节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模路由常需按指定顺序访问节点簇，即 OCTSP。独立优化每个簇看似自然，但会忽略簇间的非局部依赖，因此需要一种既能利用簇结构、又能捕捉全局依赖的求解方法。

Method: COMPASS 将搜索与学习加速的路由相结合，通过编排多个并行子求解器协同工作；它显式利用聚类结构，使求解复杂度随簇规模而非实例规模指数增长，从而可达到精确解；同时支持通用距离矩阵输入，不限于坐标型输入。

Result: 在实验中 COMPASS 一致优于对比方法；可扩展到 10 万个合成节点，以及 2.85 万个真实电商节点，后者据称是非对称距离下已报道的最大规模路由解，超出既有 ATSP 基准约 9 倍；其解质量随算力提升而持续改进，且无质量上限。

Conclusion: COMPASS 为 OCTSP 提供了一种无质量上限、可随算力扩展且支持通用距离矩阵的大规模路由方案，在合成与真实电商场景中验证了显著的规模与性能优势。

Abstract: Large-scale routing often requires visiting clusters of nodes in a prescribed order, giving rise to the Ordered Clustered Traveling Salesman Problem (OCTSP). Optimizing each cluster independently seems natural, but misses non-local dependencies. We introduce the COMPASS algorithm for OCTSP, which combines search with learning-accelerated routing by orchestrating parallel sub-solvers. COMPASS has no quality ceiling and its solutions keep improving with compute. It exploits the clustered structure, and can reach exact solutions in time exponential in cluster size rather than instance size. Empirically, COMPASS consistently outperforms alternative methods. Unlike common large-scale routing solvers, COMPASS consumes general distance matrices and is not limited to coordinate inputs. We demonstrate scaling to 100K synthetic nodes and to 28.5K real e-commerce nodes. To our knowledge, the latter is the largest reported routing solution over asymmetric distances, 9x beyond established ATSP benchmarks.

</details>


### [259] [Minimax-Optimal Online Contract Design with Unrestricted Bounded Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20353)
*Rui Ai,David Simchi-Levi,Han Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究重复契约设计中的极小极大遗憾，证明在 m≥2 种结果时 T 轮遗憾为 T^{m/(m+1)} 量级，并给出达到该速率的学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 委托人只能观测结果而无法观测代理人的行动，且代理人的最优响应可能导致期望利润对支付的依赖不连续，因此需要研究在这种信息不对称与不连续性下的重复契约设计的最优学习代价。

Method: 采用极小极大遗憾框架，允许任意有界结果依赖支付向量、任意行动空间与代理人异质性，不假设平滑性或单调剩余。通过有效维度约简，在固定打破平局规则非平移不变时也能规范化基准，并利用显示偏好得到支付差坐标下的单调响应映射，再基于该映射的 Lipschitz 参数化构造仅依赖观测结果类别的学习策略；下界构造则刻画激励损失如何随结果维度累积。

Result: 对任意固定结果数 m≥2，T 轮极小极大遗憾为 T^{m/(m+1)}（忽略对数因子）量级；上界策略达到该速率，下界证明每增加一个可契约结果都会带来精确且不可避免的最坏情形学习成本增长。

Conclusion: 重复契约学习的最优速率随结果数量增加而变差，且该结论在较弱的假设下成立，揭示了可契约结果维度对契约设计学习难度的根本影响。

Abstract: We study repeated contract design when a principal observes outcomes but not the actions that generate them. The principal may use any bounded outcome-contingent payment vector, and the agent's best response can make expected profit discontinuous in those payments. For every fixed number $m\ge2$ of outcomes, the minimax regret over $T$ rounds is of order $T^{m/(m+1)}$, up to logarithmic factors. The upper bound allows arbitrary action spaces and agent heterogeneity, without smoothness or monotone-surplus assumptions. Its key is an effective-dimension reduction that the benchmark can be normalized even when fixed tie-breaking is not shift invariant, after which revealed preference yields a monotone response map in payment-difference coordinates. A learning policy built on a Lipschitz parametrization of this map attains the rate using only observed outcome categories. The lower-bound construction accounts for how incentive losses accumulate across outcome dimensions. It shows that each additional contractible outcome creates a precise and unavoidable increase in the worst-case cost of learning.

</details>


### [260] [Learning Principal-Agent Contracts for Equitable Smallholder Carbon Farming under Moral Hazard and Adverse Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20404)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Yadati Narahari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将小农碳农业合约设计建模为POMDP，用强化学习求解动态利润最大化合约，发现利润最大化聚合商会放大而非缓解对小农的排斥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业土壤是尚未开发的巨大碳汇，碳农业具有减排潜力。南亚和撒哈拉以南非洲以小农为主，是规模化碳农业的关键，但现实中的碳项目却难以覆盖他们。作者希望从合约设计视角解释并缓解这一缺口。

Method: 构建聚合商向异质小农群体提供单一池化合约的模型，农户具有私人采纳成本（逆向选择）和不可观测努力（道德风险），农艺结果跨多个季节演化。将该演化合约问题形式化为POMDP，并用强化学习学习动态利润最大化合约，比较不同条件下的聚合商表现，并做MRV成本与面积成比例的反事实模拟。

Result: 利润最大化聚合商不仅继承了小农被排斥的现象，还将其放大：大农场上实现87.7%的可实现采纳率，小农场上仅8.2%。每公顷MRV成本随农场规模上升而下降，池化合约进一步强化而非抵消这一梯度；若使MRV成本纯粹与面积成比例，该差异可被消除。

Conclusion: 研究揭示合约与MRV成本结构如何系统性地排斥小农，可为合约和政策设计提供依据，使小农获得碳收益，同时推动农业土壤大规模参与气候减缓。

Abstract: Agricultural soils are a major untapped carbon sink. Carbon farming is emerging as a promising practice for tapping this potential. Smallholder farmers, who dominate agriculture across South Asia and sub-Saharan Africa, are key to scaling climate mitigation via carbon farming. It is ironic that real-world carbon programs largely fail to reach them. We study this important gap through the lens of contract design. An aggregator offers a single pooled contract to a heterogeneous population of smallholder farmers who have private adoption costs (adverse selection) and exert unobserved effort (moral hazard), with agronomic outcomes evolving over multiple seasons. We formulate this evolving contracting problem as a POMDP and use reinforcement learning to learn a dynamic profit-maximising contract. We analyse the performance of the aggregator under various conditions. We find that a profit-maximising aggregator does not merely inherit the exclusion of smallholders, it amplifies it. On large farms the aggregator realises 87.7% of achievable adoption, against only 8.2% on smallholdings. Per-hectare Measurement, Reporting and Verification (MRV) costs fall as farm size rises, and the aggregator's pooling contract compounds this gradient rather than offsetting it. A counterfactual that makes MRV costs purely area-proportional eliminates this disparity. Our results and simulation can guide contract and policy design that opens carbon income to smallholders while enabling agricultural soils to contribute to climate mitigation at scale.

</details>


### [261] [The Bias of Nonlinear Two-Time-scale Stochastic Approximation under Constant Step-Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20409)
*Djamel Rassem Lamouri,Dorian Baudry,Nicolas Gast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究常步长下非线性双时间尺度随机逼近（TTSA）的有限时间误差界，证明均方误差与偏差上界为 $O(\alpha+\beta^2/\alpha^2)$，并在 $\beta\le\alpha^{3/2}$ 时证明其紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时间尺度随机逼近是分析强化学习、优化与随机控制中耦合迭代算法的基本工具，但非线性情形的有限时间保证（尤其是常步长情形）仍难以获得。

Method: 在标准稳定性、正则性与马尔可夫噪声假设下，研究步长满足 $\alpha\gg\beta$ 的非线性 TTSA，对两个迭代量围绕其极限均衡的均方误差与偏差给出上界，并将误差来源分解为初始条件、快时间尺度跟踪误差、马尔可夫依赖与时间尺度耦合四部分。

Result: 误差上界为 $O(\alpha+\beta^2/\alpha^2)$，且在 $\beta\le\alpha^{3/2}$ 时该界是紧的；分析澄清了 $\beta^2/\alpha^2$ 项的来源。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了与已有线性 TTSA 情形的定性差异，表明非线性动力学引入了线性情形中不存在的额外有限时间效应。

Abstract: Two-timescale stochastic approximation (TTSA) is a fundamental tool for analyzing coupled iterative algorithms in reinforcement learning, optimization, and stochastic control. However, finite-time guarantees for nonlinear two-timescale schemes remain difficult to obtain, especially under constant step-sizes. In this paper, we study nonlinear TTSA with step-sizes $α\ggβ$. Under standard stability, regularity, and Markovian noise assumptions, we upper bound the mean-squared error and the bias of both iterates around their limiting equilibria. Our bounds scale as $O(α+β^2/α^2)$, which we prove to be tight when $β\leα^{3/2}$. The analysis separates the contributions of initial conditions, fast-timescale tracking error, Markovian dependence, and timescale coupling, thereby clarifying the origin of the $β^2/α^2$ term. Our results reveal qualitative differences from the linear TTSA setting previously studied, showing that nonlinear dynamics introduce additional finite-time effects that are absent in the linear case.

</details>


### [262] [SCGFM-ART: Amortized Relational Transport for Structure-Centric Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20419)
*Xiaodong He,Xincheng Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCGFM-ART，一种以结构为中心的图基础模型框架，通过摊销关系传输（ART）将任意图对齐到共享的关系地图集，实现跨异构图域的可迁移表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型需在拓扑、规模与特征语义差异极大的图域间学习可迁移表示，但严重的域偏移使构建统一、域无关的表示空间变得困难。

Method: 以有限关系地标（基）定义通用坐标系统“关系地图集”，ART 直接预测可复用的端到端图到基的传输方案，避免运行时昂贵的 Gromov-Wasserstein 优化；将图分解为全局关系响应坐标与局部节点到角色的结构对应，把异构节点属性投影到规范角色空间。理论上将图与地图集基建模为有限测度关系空间，给出坐标保真界、预测传输方案下的稳定性证明及摊销覆盖界。

Result: 在 14 个跨域图级与节点级分类任务上取得最优迁移性能，平均排名分别为 2.29 和 1.14；拓扑扰动分析表明节点-角色传输保留了超越全局坐标的细粒度结构信息；真实基准上冻结目标域推理速度提升 44.2 至 85.1 倍。

Conclusion: SCGFM-ART 通过结构中心的对齐与摊销传输，有效缓解图域间结构与语义异构问题，兼顾可迁移性、理论保证与推理效率。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable representations across severely heterogeneous graph domains. However, severe domain shifts in topology, graph scale, and feature semantics impede the construction of a unified, domain-agnostic representation space. To address this, we propose SCGFM-ART, a structure-centric GFM framework that aligns arbitrary graphs onto a shared relational atlas via Amortized Relational Transport (ART). The relational atlas serves as a universal coordinate system defined by a finite set of relational landmarks (bases), while ART directly predicts reusable, end-to-end graph-to-base transport plans, bypassing costly runtime Gromov-Wasserstein optimizations. Under this formulation, SCGFM-ART decomposes a graph into a unified representation: globally via its relational response coordinates relative to the atlas, and locally via its node-to-role structural correspondences. These correspondences project disparate node attributes into a canonical role space, resolving structural and semantic heterogeneity within a singular alignment interface. Rigorously modeling graphs and atlas bases as finite measured relational spaces, we establish coordinate fidelity bounds, prove stability under predicted transport plans, and derive an amortized coverage bound that guarantees our learning objective tightly surrogates ideal relational coverage. Benchmarked across 14 cross-domain graph- and node-level classification tasks, SCGFM-ART achieves state-of-the-art transferability, securing superior average ranks of 2.29 and 1.14, respectively. Topological perturbation analyses demonstrate that node-role transport retains fine-grained structural nuances beyond global coordinates. On real-world benchmarks, the amortized formulation yields 44.2 to 85.1 times faster frozen target-domain inference by avoiding iterative alignment at test time.

</details>


### [263] [Seismic Site Response Prediction from Sparse Observations Using Finite-Element-Pretrained Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20451)
*Yi Zhu,Su Chen,Xiaojun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE-T框架，通过从有限元模拟中学习低维响应流形与输入驱动动力学，并利用少量实测记录校准潜空间动力学，从而改进数值场地响应预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值场地响应预测常与实测观测存在偏差，但由于传感器覆盖有限、事件数量有限，直接修正这些偏差十分困难。因此需要一种数据高效的方法，将稠密数值模拟信息与有限实测记录结合，以提升未来场地响应预测精度。

Method: 提出Transfer-Enabled Forced Latent Autoencoder for Response Equations（FLARE-T）。该框架先从稠密有限元模拟中学习低维响应流形和输入驱动动力学；再训练稀疏编码器将模拟传感器响应映射到学得的潜坐标中；最后利用有限记录在潜空间内校准动力学。预测时用短响应窗口初始化，并由完整基底运动驱动响应。

Result: 在分层土离心机试验和Lotung现场竖向台阵上评估。测试集结果表明，FLARE-T相较于原始有限元模型，改进了多深度加速度时程和5%阻尼伪加速度反应谱，在不同强度运动下降低了每个评估传感器的误差；在Lotung还降低了两个水平分量的误差。两种不同本构参数的Lotung源模型取得了相当的测试集精度，说明对精确先验标定的依赖降低。

Conclusion: FLARE-T提供了一种数据高效的方法，将稠密数值响应信息与有限现场记录相结合，可改进未来场地响应预测，并减少对精确先验模型标定的依赖。

Abstract: Numerical site-response predictions often deviate from observations, yet correcting these discrepancies is difficult because records are limited in both sensor coverage and number of events. This study proposes the Transfer-Enabled Forced Latent Autoencoder for Response Equations (FLARE-T) to improve these predictions by learning and calibrating low-dimensional latent dynamics that connect the base acceleration input to acceleration outputs at multiple depths. FLARE-T learns a low-dimensional response manifold and input-driven dynamics from dense finite-element simulations. It then trains a sparse encoder to map simulated sensor responses into the learned coordinates and uses limited records to calibrate the dynamics within them. A short response window initializes each prediction, while the complete base motion drives the response. The framework was evaluated using a layered-soil centrifuge test and the Lotung field vertical array. Test-set results show that FLARE-T improved multi-depth acceleration histories and 5%-damped pseudoacceleration response spectra relative to the original finite-element models, reducing errors at every evaluated sensor for motions of different intensities and, at Lotung, for both horizontal components. Two Lotung source models with different constitutive parameters achieved comparable test-set accuracy, indicating reduced dependence on precise prior calibration. FLARE-T therefore provides a data-efficient means of combining dense numerical response information with limited field records to improve future site-response predictions.

</details>


### [264] [Don't Mask the Environment: Observation Supervision Changes How Agents Explore Under RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20715)
*Juzheng Zhang,Disha Makhija,Manoj Ghuhan Arivazhagan,Vinayshekhar Bannihatti Kumar,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActObs，在 agent 轨迹的 SFT 中不仅监督动作 token，也监督观测 token，从而在不增加数据、参数、序列长度或前向计算的情况下提升后续强化学习的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SFT 只对智能体生成的动作 token 计算损失，将环境观测仅作为上下文而非预测目标。作者质疑这一惯例是否为后续强化学习提供了最佳初始化，认为只监督动作会导致策略单方面特化、丧失对动作后果的建模能力。

Method: ActObs 在 SFT 阶段同时监督轨迹中已存在的观测 token，让策略学习预测动作带来的环境后果；该做法不增加数据、参数、序列 token 或前向计算。随后在 GRPO 强化学习阶段，将 ActObs 初始化与纯动作监督初始化进行对比，并在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 与 aider-polyglot 跨域代码编辑任务上评估。

Result: 两种方法在 SFT 后表现相近，但在 GRPO 后出现分化。Qwen3-4B 上，ActObs 初始化在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 各采样预算下 pass@k 均更高；Qwen3-8B 上以少量 pass@1 可靠性换取更高 pass@k（pass@16 提升 3.4 个百分点）并解决更多不同任务。跨域代码编辑 aider-polyglot 上 4B 模型 pass@1 提升 4.2 个百分点。ActObs 在 RL 中保留更多熵、所需策略移动更少，最终策略更接近 SFT 初始化。

Conclusion: 分析表明差异源于 SFT 阶段：动作与观测梯度迅速趋于正交，仅监督动作会留下较大的观测梯度残差，使环境预测能力低于基座模型。联合监督可防止这种单向特化，保留后果预测能力并为下游探索做好准备。

Abstract: Agent trajectories record what an agent does and what happens next. Yet standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies loss only to agent-authored action tokens, using environment observations as context but not as prediction targets. We ask whether this convention provides the best initialization for subsequent reinforcement learning. We introduce ActObs, which also supervises the observation tokens already present in each trajectory. Although deployed agents never generate observations, learning to predict them encourages the policy to model action consequences without adding data, parameters, sequence tokens, or forward passes. The methods perform similarly after SFT but diverge after GRPO. On Qwen3-4B, GRPO from ActObs achieves higher pass@k at every evaluated sampling budget than its action-only counterpart on Terminal-Bench 2.0. On Qwen3-8B, it trades some pass@1 reliability for higher pass@k (+3.4 pp at pass@16) and solves more distinct tasks. The advantage extends to cross-domain code editing on aider-polyglot (+4.2 pp at pass@1 at 4B), whose tasks are unseen during SFT and RL. ActObs retains more entropy during RL while requiring less policy movement, leaving the final policy closer to its SFT initialization. Our analysis traces this difference to SFT: action and observation gradients rapidly become orthogonal, while action-only training leaves a large residual observation gradient and degrades environment prediction below the base model. Joint supervision prevents this one-sided specialization, preserving consequence prediction and preparing the policy for downstream exploration.

</details>


### [265] [Training Neural Networks to Approach the Optimum Bayes Estimator in Dense Multi-Emitter Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20465)
*Yi Sun,Mona Sharifi,Muzna Yumman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在合成帧上训练神经网络，以逼近密集发射体定位的贝叶斯估计器最优解，为高通量大视场超时空分辨率SMLM的后续研究提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集发射体定位问题中，传统贝叶斯估计器虽为最优解但计算复杂、难以实用。作者希望借助神经网络学习该估计器，以突破计算效率瓶颈，并面向高通量、大视场的超时空分辨率单分子定位显微（SMLM）需求。

Method: 使用合成的图像帧（synthesized frames）作为训练数据，训练神经网络来逼近密集发射体定位的贝叶斯估计器（Bayes estimator）。

Result: 训练后的神经网络能够逼近密集发射体定位的贝叶斯估计器最优解，验证了该方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持未来进一步开展利用神经网络实现高通量、大视场超时空分辨率SMLM的工作。

Abstract: We train neural networks on synthesized frames to approach the optimum Bayes estimator for dense emitter localization. The result justifies the future work on training neural networks to achieve high-throughput large-FOV super spatiotemporal resolution SMLM.

</details>


### [266] [Deep Learning-Based Classification of Cognitive and Resting States Using Electroencephalography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20467)
*K. A. Januka S. Fernando,Harshit Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种融合CNN与GRU的深度学习框架，结合时频分析对EEG信号进行特征提取，用于区分静息状态与认知状态，在多个二分类任务上取得约76%–83%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从EEG信号中识别认知与静息状态对理解大脑活动波动具有重要意义。EEG是非侵入式记录大脑功能的手段，而深度学习可自动从复杂EEG数据中提取显著模式，因此有必要构建有效的自动分类框架。

Method: 研究构建了一个深度学习框架：将卷积神经网络（CNN）与门控循环单元（GRU）堆叠，用于EEG信号特征提取；同时进行时频分析以探索信号显著特征，并将提取的特征交由传统深度学习与机器学习分类器（包括所提出的2D-Net架构）进行评估。

Result: 所提方法与特征提取策略优于对比方法：静息-数学任务分类准确率为83.177%，静息-记忆任务分类准确率为76.107%，静息-音乐任务分类准确率为83.432%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将信号处理与深度学习方法相结合，能够有效利用EEG信号区分静息状态与认知状态。

Abstract: The categorization of cognitive and resting states derived from electroencephalography (EEG) signals is crucial for comprehending fluctuations in brain activity linked to various mental states. EEG provides a non-intrusive approach for documenting brain function in both resting and task-oriented cognitive conditions, whilst deep learning techniques enable the automatic extraction of significant patterns from intricate EEG data. This study presents a deep learning framework to distinguish between resting and cognitive states through EEG records. The proposed framework integrates a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) stacked with a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) for the extraction of features from EEG signals. Time-frequency analysis is conducted to explore the salient aspects of signals, and the derived features are then assessed utilizing conventional deep learning and machine learning classifiers, including the suggested 2D-Net architecture. The proposed approach and feature extraction strategy outperform the evaluated comparative methods, achieving accuracies of 83.177% for resting-versus-mathematical task classification, 76.107% for resting-versus-memory task classification, and 83.432% for resting-versus-music task classification. The findings illustrate the efficacy of integrating signal processing with deep learning methodologies to discriminate resting from cognitive states utilizing EEG signals.

</details>


### [267] [Distributionally Robust Federated Learning with Multi-Source Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20501)
*Yingzhu Liu,Zhongkui Li,Pengcheng You,Ashish Cherukuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中跨客户端混合比例不确定与客户端内部数据分布模糊的双重问题，构建了局部模糊集的全局并集模糊集，并设计了具有收敛保证的联邦算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习从各客户端私有数据中训练共享模型，但现实中各客户端的数据生成分布往往不同，且真实的跨客户端混合权重常未知，导致难以准确刻画整体群组分布。现有方法通过优化最坏情况混合来应对跨客户端混合不确定性，但依赖准确的客户端级分布估计，而有限样本下这种估计并不可靠。

Method: 作者同时考虑跨客户端混合不确定性和客户端内部的分布模糊性，构建一个全局模糊集，将其定义为各局部模糊集的可容许混合的并集；该构造允许各客户端使用不同的模糊半径，并可转化为按客户端可分离的优化形式。在此基础上，作者建立了高概率的样本外性能保证，并针对惩罚式重构开发了联邦算法，在较温和的正则条件下证明了其收敛性。

Result: 理论上给出了高概率样本外性能保证以及算法的收敛性证明；仿真实验验证了所提联邦算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内部分布模糊性统一纳入全局模糊集并实现可分重构，该方法在有限样本下更具鲁棒性，且配套算法兼具理论保证与实证有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning trains a shared model from private client data. In practice, data-generating distributions may differ, and the true mixture across clients is often unknown, making the underlying group distribution difficult to specify. Existing approaches address cross-client mixture uncertainty by optimizing against the worst-case mixture, yet assume accurate client-wise distribution estimates. However, these estimates can be unreliable when based on finite samples. To handle both cross-client mixture uncertainty and within-client distributional ambiguity, we construct a global ambiguity set as the union of admissible mixtures of local ambiguity sets. The construction allows client-specific ambiguity radii and admits a client-wise separable reformulation. Leveraging this structure, we establish a high-probability out-of-sample performance guarantee. We further develop a federated algorithm for a penalty-based reformulation and prove its convergence under milder regularity conditions. Simulations validate the algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [268] [Radio Frequency Detection and Classification of Microplastics in Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20507)
*Jaden Tolbert,Md Saiful Islam,Pingshan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机器学习辅助射频介电光谱流式技术，实现水环境中微纳塑料的无标记检测与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微纳塑料污染日益严重，传统光学与光谱方法在低微米尺度下受通量低、样品制备复杂等限制，亟需快速无标记检测手段。

Method: 搭建ML辅助的射频介电光谱流式细胞平台DiSC，对去离子水中8类约10微米塑料颗粒在0.2–9 GHz四个频点测量S参数变化，并以载体介质为参照训练监督式ML分类模型，测试混合样品与盐水环境。

Result: 去离子水中8类微塑料的宏平均F1、精确率与召回率均超过0.71；在含3.3%和6.6%海盐的盐水载体中，PET分类性能基本保持。

Conclusion: ML辅助RF DiSC在水相单颗粒微塑料分类中具有可行性；未来将通过改进射频校准、扩展频谱覆盖、扩大训练集及环境老化与生物污染样本验证提升性能。

Abstract: Micro- and nano-plastic particles (MPs/NPs) are ubiquitous environmental contaminants whose increasing abundance and potential health impacts have created an urgent need for rapid, label-free detection methods. As particle size decreases to the low-micrometer range, conventional optical and spectroscopic techniques become increasingly challenging because of limited throughput and/or complex sample preparation. In this work, we present a machine learning (ML)-assisted radio-frequency (RF) dielectric spectroscopic cytometry (DiSC) platform for the label-free detection and classification of MPs. Eight types of $ 10 $ μm nominal-diameter MP particles suspended in deionized (DI) water were characterized at four frequencies spanning $ 0.2\text{-}9\text{ GHz} $. The measured alterations in RF scattering parameters (S-parameters), referenced to the carrier medium, were used to train supervised ML models for material classification, including the identification of MPs in mixed samples and saline-water environments. For eight MP classes suspended in DI water, the proposed method achieved macro-average F1-score, precision, and recall values exceeding $ 0.71 $. Furthermore, PET classification performance was largely maintained in saline carrier media containing $3.3\% $ and $ 6.6\% $ sea salt. These results demonstrate the feasibility of ML-assisted RF DiSC for rapid, single-particle MP classification in aqueous environments. Future work will focus on improving classification performance through enhanced RF calibration, increased spectral coverage, larger training datasets, and validation using environmentally aged and biologically contaminated microplastics.

</details>


### [269] [When EOS Tokens Disagree: Understanding Length Inflation in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20511)
*Yuxiao Yang,Tianrun Yu,Shangzhe Li,Kaixiang Zhao,Xuchao Zhang,Chetan Bansal,Huaxiu Yao,Taylor W. Killian,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的"长度膨胀"问题，指出基座学生模型与后训练教师模型之间的"终止符不匹配"是重要成因，并提出将功能等价的EOS符视为共享语义终止动作的方法来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线策略蒸馏中，学生模型的回复会变得过长，甚至耗尽生成预算。作者希望找出这种长度膨胀的成因。作者发现基座学生与后训练教师可能在不同的EOS符上放置停止概率，即使二者声明的停止集合相同；这种不匹配会抑制学生偏好的终止动作，又无法可靠迁移教师偏好的替代动作。

Method: 作者在Qwen3、Llama和Gemma三个模型家族上分析终止符不匹配现象，验证仅对齐解码停止集合效果不足，进而提出将功能等价的EOS符视为一个共享的语义终止动作。此外，作者在OPD的不同K2-Horizon训练阶段进行分阶段分析，观察终止偏好的演化。

Result: 将功能等价的EOS符作为共享语义终止动作，能在三个模型家族上显著缓解由不匹配引起的长度膨胀；分阶段分析显示终止偏好在训练中会发生明显漂移，并且在OPD后期还存在一种超越终止对齐的、独特的长度膨胀现象。

Conclusion: 终止符不匹配是OPD长度动态的一个重要但非唯一来源。作者发布了包含所提终止处理修正的实现。

Abstract: We study length inflation in on-policy distillation (OPD), where student responses can become excessively long and even exhaust the generation budget. We identify \emph{termination-token mismatch} between base students and post-trained teachers as an important source of this behavior. Across Qwen3, Llama, and Gemma, the two models can place their stopping probability on different EOS tokens, even when their declared stopping sets are identical. This mismatch can suppress the student's preferred termination action without reliably transferring the teacher-preferred alternative. We show that aligning the decoding stopping set alone is insufficient, while treating functionally equivalent EOS tokens as a shared semantic stopping action substantially mitigates mismatch-induced length inflation across all three model families. To further understand how termination behavior evolves over training, we study OPD across different K2-Horizon training stages. This stage-wise analysis shows that termination preferences can shift substantially during training, while also revealing a distinct length inflation late in the OPD run that persists beyond termination alignment. Together, these results identify termination mismatch as an important, but not exhaustive, source of OPD length dynamics. We release an implementation incorporating the proposed termination-handling corrections.

</details>


### [270] [Parallelism, critical windows, and separations among diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20539)
*Sitan Chen,Liye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对掩码、均匀和高斯三类扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的并行性进行细粒度理论比较，证明了均匀/高斯扩散可在与对偶总相关性相关的正向传播次数内采样，并首次给出三类范式的可证明并行性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型以并行生成为卖点，但掩码、均匀、高斯三种主流扩散范式在并行性上的优劣缺乏原理性理解，此前仅知掩码扩散能达到基于内在复杂度的采样效率。

Method: 从理论上比较三种dLLM范式的并行能力：分析正向传播次数与分布对偶总相关性（内在复杂度度量）的关系，并针对一类随机经验测度证明均匀/高斯扩散的复杂度上界与下界，同时构造掩码扩散的近似评分预言机反例。

Result: 均匀与高斯扩散的采样正向传播次数可随对偶总相关性缩放（此前仅掩码扩散已知可达到）；对某类随机经验测度，均匀/高斯扩散需且仅需Õ(√d)次正向传播，而掩码扩散在某些近似评分预言机下需要Õ(d)次，首次建立了三者的可证明并行性分离。

Conclusion: 并行性差异并非源于掩码扩散必须确定词元值的直觉，而是因为掩码扩散采样中的关键窗口渐近地比均匀和高斯扩散更窄。

Abstract: A popular selling point of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is their capacity for parallelism: the ability to generate sequences of text far more efficiently than autoregressive models, which require one forward pass per token. Yet among the many competing paradigms for dLLMs, from masked to uniform to Gaussian diffusion, principled understanding of how these different proposals compare in parallelism remains limited. In this work, we initiate a fine-grained comparison of the capacity for parallelism among these three leading approaches and prove the following:
  - Uniform and Gaussian diffusion can sample in a number of forward passes which scales with the dual total correlation of the underlying distribution, a measure of intrinsic complexity which can be much smaller than the context length. Previously, it was only known how to achieve this using masked diffusion.
  - For a certain family of random empirical measures, we show that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{d})$ forward passes are necessary and sufficient to sample using uniform or Gaussian diffusion, yet there exist approximate score oracles for which $\widetildeΩ(d)$ forward passes are needed for masked diffusion. This establishes the first provable separation in parallelism between the three prevailing dLLM paradigms.
  Contrary to popular intuition that masked diffusions are harder to parallelize because they must commit to token values, the latter separation instead comes from the fact that the critical windows in masked diffusion sampling are asymptotically narrower than those in uniform and Gaussian diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [271] [Mitigating Retaliatory Algorithmic Collusion in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20548)
*Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究重复博弈中强化学习智能体自发形成的默契合谋问题，提出CURB方法通过奖励塑形消除合谋均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体在重复交互中最大化自身奖励时，即使没有通信或共享设计，也可能收敛到超竞争结果（类似显式合谋）。现有缓解方法多局限于特定经济场景（如双边平台、拍卖），缺乏针对一般重复博弈的干预设计。

Method: 作者将Q学习合谋的实证观察与经典Simple Penal Codes（SPC）理论联系，证明任何非平凡SPC都会在智能体策略中引入可量化的条件依赖，可通过合作历史与背叛历史下动作分布的总变差距离检测。基于此提出CURB框架，在Q学习过程中对总变差（TV）距离信号进行惩罚，理论上保证将任何SPC不动点转化为平凡不动点，从而排除由惩罚威胁维持的合谋均衡。

Result: 在Bertrand和Cournot竞争重复博弈中，CURB显著减少了Q学习智能体的合谋行为；并在Bertrand竞争中的深度Q网络（DQN）智能体上也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: CURB通过奖励塑形与信念注入机制，为一般重复博弈提供了有理论保证的反合谋干预手段，且机制可推广至表格型Q学习之外。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents trained to maximize their own reward in repeated interactions can converge to supra-competitive outcomes resembling explicit collusion, without communication or shared design. Existing mitigation approaches are largely tied to specific economic settings, like two-sided platforms and auctions, leaving open how to design interventions for general repeated games. We address this gap by formalizing the connection between empirical observations from prior work on Q-learning collusion and classical theory of Simple Penal Codes (SPCs). We show any non-trivial SPC induces a quantifiable conditional dependence in agents' policies, detectable via the total variation distance between an agent's action distributions across cooperation and defection histories. Building on this connection, we propose CURB (Collusion Unwinding via Reward shaping and Belief injection), a reward-shaping framework that penalizes this Total Variation (TV) distance signal during Q-learning and is guaranteed to convert any SPC fixed point of the dynamics into a trivial one, thus precluding collusive equilibria sustained by punishment threats. Empirically, CURB substantially reduces collusion by Q-learning agents in both Bertrand and Cournot Competition Repeated Games. We further demonstrate that CURB extends to deep Q-network agents in Bertrand competition, suggesting the mechanism generalizes beyond tabular Q-learning.

</details>


### [272] [CrystalMO-TuRBO: Multi-Objective Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization for High-precision Joint Crystal Structure Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20592)
*Joseph Agada,Yishu Wang,Arpan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CrystalMO-TuRBO，一种多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化架构，用于X射线与中子衍射数据的联合晶体结构精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体结构精修方法（最小二乘、似然优化）依赖局部搜索，在非凸、含噪、参数高度相关的场景下表现不佳；尤其X射线与中子联合精修时，两者灵敏度互补但相互竞争，通常以标量化目标配合人工权重组合，导致解次优。

Method: 将X射线与中子偏差分别建模为独立目标，转化为归一化最大化问题；采用两阶段优化：第一阶段用并行信赖域贝叶斯优化在多种标量化下进行全局探索，第二阶段在收缩区域内局部精修，从而显式分离全局搜索与精细优化。

Result: 在单晶Ho2Ti2O7的实验X射线与中子衍射数据上评估，结果显示在收敛性、鲁棒性和参数精度上均优于经典精修方法与贝叶斯优化基线。

Conclusion: 该多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化框架能有效应对联合晶体结构精修中的非凸与竞争性目标难题，提升精修精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Crystal structure refinement is a fundamental inverse problem in materials characterization, where structural parameters are optimized to reproduce experimental diffraction data. Conventional approaches, such as least-squares and likelihood-based optimization, rely on local search and often struggle with non-convex, noisy, and highly correlated parameter landscapes, particularly when integrating multiple diffraction modalities. Joint refinement of X-ray and neutron data is especially challenging due to their complementary but competing sensitivities, which are typically combined through scalarized objectives requiring manual weighting and leading to suboptimal solutions. We propose CrystalMO-TuRBO, a multi-objective trust region Bayesian optimization architecture for joint crystal structure refinement. The method models X-ray and neutron discrepancies as separate objectives and transforms the problem into a normalized maximization setting. A two-phase optimization strategy is introduced: Phase 1 performs global exploration using parallel trust-region Bayesian optimization across multiple scalarizations to identify promising regions of the parameter space, while Phase 2 conducts localized refinement within a shrinking region to achieve high-precision solutions. This design explicitly separates global search from fine-grained optimization, addressing the unique accuracy requirements of refinement tasks. We evaluate the proposed method on experimentally collected X-ray and neutron diffraction data from single-crystal Ho2Ti2O7. Results demonstrate improved convergence, robustness, and parameter precision compared to classical refinement methods and Bayesian optimization baselines on refinement of a single-crystal pyrochlore material system.

</details>


### [273] [Recursive Quantum Long Short-Term Memory for Stable Short-Horizon Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20594)
*Mu-En Lee,Yen-Ku Liu,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较递归型QLSTM与标准QLSTM在气温预测任务上的优化与泛化表现，发现递归结构收敛更快、误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子长短期记忆（QLSTM）模型将变分量子电路引入循环序列学习，但其优化行为会随随机初始化和时间上下文发生显著变化，稳定性与泛化性存在不确定性，因此需要评估不同架构的优化表现。

Method: 使用多伦多每日天气观测数据，在相同训练设置下对比递归QLSTM与标准QLSTM的单步气温（日最低与最高温）预测；输入窗口设为8、16、32天，并在20个随机种子上比较收敛性、预测精度和泛化能力。

Result: 递归模型一致地更早达到接近最优的测试损失，平均绝对误差和均方根误差更低，泛化差距也更小。

Conclusion: 递归量子特征变换能够提升紧凑型混合量子—经典时序模型的稳定性与样本外性能。该工作属于"Other"，不属于LLM或MLLM。

Abstract: Quantum long short-term memory (QLSTM) models extend recurrent sequence learning with variational quantum circuits, but their optimization behavior can vary substantially across random initializations and temporal contexts. This paper evaluates a recursive QLSTM architecture against a standard QLSTM for one-step-ahead prediction of daily minimum and maximum temperature. Using daily weather observations from Toronto and identical training settings, we compare convergence, predictive accuracy, and generalization across input windows of 8, 16, and 32 days over 20 random seeds. The recursive model consistently reaches a near-optimal test loss earlier, reduces mean absolute error and root mean squared error, and exhibits a smaller generalization gap. These results indicate that recursive quantum feature transformations can improve stability and out-of-sample performance for compact hybrid quantum--classical temporal models.

</details>


### [274] [Multi-center Medical Data Mining with FL-Net - A One-stop Shop for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20650)
*Simon Süwer,Julian Klemm,Elisa Acitelli,Mathieu Almeida,Lucia Altucci,Zsolt Bagyura,Michelangela Barbieri,Zsolt-Zoltán Bedő,Rosaria Benedetti,Béla Bihari,Csongor Csalóka,Lucia Dicunta,Stanislav Ehrlich,Bjoern M. Eskofier,Sándor-József Fejér,Georg Fröwis,Walter Hötzendorfer,Alexandra Kautzky-Willer,Jens Johann Georg Lohmann,Marianna Maranghi,Lorenzo Marconi,Rudolf Mayer,Wouter Leonard Megchelenbrink,Monika Moga,Adham Mottalib,Sanjeev Mehta,Madeleine Müller,Thomas Nyström,Balázs-Attila Orbán,Paul O'Toole,Giuseppe Paolisso,Paolo Parini,Matteo Pedrelli,Enrico Petrillo,Philipp Poindl,Niklas Probul,Anastasia Pustozerova,Tanja Šarčević,Lukas Weilguny,Jan Baumbach,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FL-Net是一个联邦临床研究框架，满足文献中提出的五项需求，集成数据协调、数据发现、披露控制、版本化工具与容器化工作流，并在MIMIC与US-130上完成端到端评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可实现无需共享患者级数据的协作训练，但多数研究停留在仿真阶段；作者基于文献提炼出五项需求，分析14个联邦学习框架后发现无一完全满足，因此需要一个实用的联邦临床研究框架。

Method: 提出FL-Net框架，将模块化数据协调、数据发现、披露控制、安全构建的版本化FL-Net-Tools以及容器化联邦工作流执行整合到持久网络中，支持协调数据和工作流的跨研究复用；通过协调、跨研究患者发现（MIMIC与US-130）以及可复现、可审计的联邦工作流（最多50个并发客户端）进行端到端评估。

Result: FL-Net在协调、跨研究患者发现和可复现可审计的联邦工作流方面完成评估，支持最多50个并发客户端；该框架正在dAIbetes和Microb-AI-ome欧盟项目中开发，将覆盖9个国家10家医院超过80万名患者，包含纵向和单时点数据。

Conclusion: FL-Net为可互操作、可复现且隐私保护的多中心临床研究提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training without sharing patient-level data, but most studies remain simulations. Based on five requirements derived from the literature, we analyzed 14 FL frameworks and found that none fully satisfied these requirements. We present FL-Net, a novel federated clinical research framework to fulfill all requirements. It integrates modular data harmonization, data discovery, disclosure control, securely built versioned FL-Net-Tools and containerized federated workflow execution into a persistent network. It enables the re-use of harmonized data and workflows across studies. FL-Net's end-to-end capabilities were evaluated through harmonization, cross-study patient discovery across MIMIC and US-130, and reproducible, audited federated workflows with up to 50 concurrent clients. FL-Net is being developed within the dAIbetes and Microb-AI-ome EU projects and will cover over 800,000 patients across 10 hospitals in 9 countries covering longitudinal and single point in time data, FL-Net provides a practical foundation for interoperable, reproducible, and privacy-preserving multicenter clinical research.

</details>


### [275] [Epidemiological Causal Graph Identification: Challenges, Identifiability and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20676)
*Sambit Mishra,Yingying Wang,Christine K. Johnson,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究含有序变量与指数族变量混合数据的DAG因果发现，证明二者间边方向在分布上可识别，并提出基于评分的穷举搜索与基于DAGMA的掩码连续优化方法，数值实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果可识别性研究多集中于连续变量与加性噪声模型，忽视了同时包含有序尺度、计数与连续测量的混合数据集。该类数据在现实中普遍存在，其因果方向的识别问题尚未被充分解决。

Method: 针对节点服从有序分布（有序logit模型）或正则单参数指数族分布的DAG，理论证明有序节点与指数族节点之间的边方向在一般参数取值下具有分布可识别性；计算上提出基于评分的穷举搜索方法，并引入基于DAGMA的掩码连续优化框架以处理较大图。

Result: 理论上将已有的Ordinal-Poisson识别结果推广至更广泛的指数族；数值实验支持理论结论，能够在马尔可夫等价类中恢复那些在经典结构方程模型下不可识别的边方向。

Conclusion: 混合有序-指数族数据的因果方向具有可识别性，所提理论推广与计算框架为更一般混合类型数据的因果发现提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is fundamental to statistics and machine learning, yet determining causal direction without interventions necessitates structural assumptions. Existing identifiability research primarily focuses on continuous variables under additive noise models, often neglecting mixed datasets containing ordinal scales, counts, and continuous measurements. This paper investigates causal discovery in Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) where nodes follow either an ordinal distribution (via an ordered logit model) or a regular one-parameter exponential family distribution. We prove that the edge direction between an ordinal and an exponential family node is distributionally identifiable for generic parameter values. Our findings generalize previous Ordinal-Poisson results to the broader exponential family. Computationally, we introduce a score-based exhaustive search and a masked continuous optimization framework using DAGMA for larger graphs. Numerical results validate the theory, recovering edge orientations within a Markov equivalence class that are unidentifiable under classical structural equation models.

</details>


### [276] [RISC-V and machine learning: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20677)
*Shriman Keshri,Apparna Singh,Chinmaya Kumar Palo,Shreya Adya,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了RISC-V指令集架构在机器学习领域的应用现状，涵盖指令集扩展、核心实现、编译器优化与部署策略，并提出四个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源处理器架构与机器学习的交叉推动了人们对可定制、高效且易获取硬件的需求，因此有必要全面评估RISC-V在这一场景下的能力与挑战。

Method: 以综述形式分析近期研究，考察学术与商业实现、软件框架及实际应用，构建统一的RISC-V机器学习实现分类体系，并对比性能与设计权衡、评估软件工具链成熟度。

Result: 研究发现RISC-V在能效、专用指令开发和框架集成方面取得进展，但存在标准化不足、验证复杂和生态碎片化等挑战。

Conclusion: 提出四个研究方向：专用神经处理扩展、自适应模块化处理器架构、安全框架以及高能效多域架构，为RISC-V作为下一代机器学习基础平台提供路线图。

Abstract: The intersection of open-source processor architectures and machine learning is driving the demand for customizable, efficient, and accessible hardware. This survey examines the state of the RISC-V ISA in machine learning applications, analyzing current capabilities, challenges, and future directions based on recent research. The analysis covers academic and commercial implementations, software frameworks, and real-world applications. The RISC-V machine learning ecosystem is evaluated, from instruction set extensions and core implementations to compiler optimizations and deployment strategies. Key contributions include a unified taxonomy of RISC-V ML implementations, a comparative analysis of performance and design trade-offs, an evaluation of software toolchain maturity, and the identification of emerging trends in instruction set extensions and specialized accelerators. Findings reveal progress in energy efficiency, specialized instruction development, and framework integration, while highlighting challenges in standardization, verification complexity, and ecosystem fragmentation. The analysis proposes four research directions to address current limitations: specialized neural processing extensions, adaptive and modular processor architectures, security frameworks, and energy-efficient multi-domain architectures. These directions provide a roadmap for advancing RISC-V as a foundational platform for next-generation machine learning systems.

</details>


### [277] [Video DeltaNet: A Video-Native Hybrid Attention for Livestream Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20744)
*Haocheng Xi,Yiming Xie,Hexu Zhao,Yiwen Zhang,Michael Liu,Thomas Creavin,Kurt Keutzer,Xiuyu Li,Zhaoyang Lv,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Video DeltaNet (VDN)，用局部 Softmax 注意力与双向线性记忆混合架构替代视频扩散模型中的全注意力，并以 Video Delta Attention 支撑长程视频建模，在 MiniMax H3 上实现 14.5 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在去噪过程中反复处理长时空 token 序列，注意力计算成为主要瓶颈；线性注意力虽在大语言模型中被广泛采用，但直接用于视频模型会丢失高质量生成所需的细粒度交互。

Method: VDN 将局部 Softmax 注意力与双向线性记忆结合：线性分支提出 Video Delta Attention (VDA)，按帧更新记忆并联合融合该帧的空间 token；两分支使用独立输出投影与可学习门控进行校准；通过分阶段教师对齐策略将新路径逐步引入预训练模型。在 MiniMax H3 上实例化 VDN-H3，在视频到视频交互中使用混合注意力，而在涉及文本或音频的交互中保留 Softmax，并采用八步蒸馏与优化的 SGLang 服务栈。

Result: 在八块 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上，VDN-H3 在 6.70 秒内完成 14.3 秒、768p 视频的 DiT 去噪，相比相同 GPU 数量下的 50 步稠密 H3 基线取得 14.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: 融合局部 Softmax 与线性记忆的混合注意力可在保持生成质量所需细粒度交互的同时大幅提升视频扩散模型效率，配合蒸馏与服务优化可实现数量级的推理加速。

Abstract: Video diffusion models repeatedly process long spatiotemporal token sequences during denoising, making attention a major computational bottleneck. Linear attention offers an appealing alternative and has been widely adopted in recent large language models, but directly applying it to video models often fails to preserve the fine-grained interactions required for high-quality generation. We present Video DeltaNet (VDN), which combines local Softmax attention with bidirectional linear memory for long-range video context. Its linear branch introduces Video Delta Attention (VDA), which updates memory once per frame by jointly incorporating its spatial tokens. Separate output projections and learnable gates calibrate the two branches, while a staged teacher-alignment recipe progressively introduces the new pathway into pretrained models. We instantiate VDN on MiniMax H3, applying the hybrid to video-to-video interactions while retaining Softmax for interactions involving text or audio. With eight-step distillation and an optimized SGLang serving stack, VDN-H3 completes DiT denoising for a 14.3-second, 768p video in 6.70 seconds on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs, corresponding to a 14.5x speedup over the 50-step dense H3 baseline on the same GPU count.

</details>


### [278] [Calibrated RF-Fingerprinting Under Interference With Heterogeneous Transmission Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20765)
*Tariq Abdul-Quddoos,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究同信道干扰下的射频指纹识别，将其建模为多标签分类问题并用一维CNN求解，同时进行模型校准以控制漏检率，在真实5G测试床上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频指纹识别研究几乎只考虑单一发射机的情形，忽略了现实世界中多信号在时间和频率上重叠、相互干扰的情况，限制了实际应用价值。

Method: 将同信道干扰下的射频指纹识别形式化为多标签分类问题，采用一维卷积神经网络（1D CNN）建模；并对模型进行校准，推导标签概率的置信阈值，给出平均假阴性数量上界的保证；使用POWDER 5G测试床的真实数据，对802.11a(Wi-Fi)、4G LTE和5G NR波形进行验证。

Result: 校准后准确率在73%至97%之间，取决于信道条件；针对不同平均假阴性上界进行校准时，micro recall分数约等于(1-校准假阴性)，且校准对分布外干扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法在真实高竞争无线环境下具有应用潜力，能够在存在同信道干扰时实现可靠的射频指纹识别并控制漏检风险。

Abstract: Radio Frequency(RF)-Fingerprinting is a spectrum monitoring technique that identifies specific transmitters based on hardware impairments imprinted within the emitted signal. Although widely researched, studies almost exclusively consider scenarios where only one transmitter is emitting at a time, limiting real world applicability. In this work, we further the study of RF-Fingerprinting by considering co-channel interference, with multiple emitted signals interfering with each other, overlapping in time and frequency. Specifically, we formulate this problem as a multi-label classification problem and employ a 1D convolutional neural network (CNN). Furthermore, the models are calibrated such that the confidence thresholds for the label probabilities are derived, with guarantees on the upper bound on the average number of False Negatives, providing a degree of confidence in not missing a true spectrum policy violation. The proposed method is validated using real world data from the POWDER 5G testbed on devices transmitting 802.11a(Wi-Fi), 4G LTE, and 5G NR waveforms. The results show accuracy as high as 97% and as low as 73% after calibration depending on channel conditions. Also calibrating for various average false negatives upper bounds achieves micro recall scores of approximately (1 - calibrated false negatives) with the calibration robust to out-of-distribution interference, demonstrating the potential of the proposed method in a realistic high contention wireless environment

</details>


### [279] [PosteriorBench: From Point Estimates to Posterior Matching in Evaluating Generative Inverse Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20794)
*Jiachen Yao,Zi-Siang Hsu,Xi Deng,Aditi Gupta,Xin Ju,Sally M Benson,Gege Wen,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PosteriorBench 基准，用高保真参考后验与五项分布度量评估生成式逆问题求解器的后验分布准确性，揭示现有方法存在显著分布匹配差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式模型求解科学逆问题的评测多聚焦于能否产生单个看似合理的重建结果。对于病态逆问题，同一稀疏或含噪观测可能对应多个解，仅凭点估计准确性无法判断方法是否真正捕捉到后验：可能出现模式坍缩、不确定性过度自信或将互不兼容的解平均化等问题。因此需要一个能评估求解器分布级准确性的基准。

Method: 构建 PosteriorBench 基准，覆盖四类基于物理的逆问题：Darcy 流反演、Poisson 源恢复、碳捕集与封存、光传输材料推断。对每个任务，使用拒绝采样、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛等高计算成本但成熟的方法构造高保真参考后验，从而可直接检验求解器是否恢复完整解集而非单一最优样本。配套五项后验评估指标：后验均值误差、后验标准差误差、最大均值差异、切片 Wasserstein 距离、径向平均功率谱误差，分别评估点估计精度、边缘不确定性、分布对齐与全局频率保真度。基准涵盖稀疏传感、低分辨率观测、非线性前向模型、不同噪声水平与多峰先验，并提供统一的分布匹配与不确定性量化流程。

Result: 实验显示，当前求解器普遍存在明显的分布匹配差距；神经算子可提升对分辨率的鲁棒性；引导权重与生成噪声是后验方差校准的关键因素。

Conclusion: 评测生成式逆问题求解器应从单点重建质量转向全后验分布准确性。PosteriorBench 通过高保真参考后验与多维分布度量提供了统一评测框架，指出分布匹配是现有方法的薄弱环节，并为改进不确定性校准指明方向。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to solve scientific inverse problems, but existing evaluations still focus primarily on whether a method can produce a single plausible reconstruction. This is insufficient for ill-posed problems, where multiple solutions may be consistent with the same sparse or noisy observations. In these settings, a method can achieve strong pointwise accuracy while still failing to capture the true posterior through mode collapse, overconfident uncertainty, or averaging incompatible solutions. We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for evaluating the distributional accuracy of generative inverse solvers. PosteriorBench evaluates four physics-based inverse problems: Darcy flow inversion, Poisson source recovery, carbon capture and storage, and light transport material inference. For each task, we construct high-fidelity reference posteriors using computationally heavy but established procedures such as rejection sampling and Markov chain Monte Carlo, enabling direct assessment of whether solvers recover the full set of solutions rather than the single best sample. We pair these references with a five-metric posterior evaluation suite: posterior-mean error, posterior-standard-deviation error, maximum mean discrepancy, sliced Wasserstein distance, and radially averaged power-spectrum error. These metrics assess pointwise accuracy, marginal uncertainty, distributional alignment, and global frequency fidelity. The benchmark spans sparse sensing, low-resolution observations, nonlinear forward models, varying noise levels, and multimodal priors, with a unified pipeline for distribution matching and uncertainty quantification. Our experiments reveal substantial distribution-matching gaps across current solvers, while showing that neural operators improve resolution robustness, and guidance weights and generation noise are key to posterior-variance calibration.

</details>


### [280] [Score Centering Stabilizes Off-policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20807)
*Martin Marek,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示大模型强化学习中训练-推理不匹配（TIM）引发的不稳定性主要源于"漂移"偏差，并提出一种可加的"分数中心化"（score centering）修正项来消除漂移、稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习对训练引擎与推理引擎之间的微小差异（训练-推理不匹配，TIM）极为敏感，导致训练不稳定。然而完全消除TIM并不现实，因为会严重损害 rollout 效率。因此需要一种在不消除TIM的前提下稳定RL训练的方法。

Method: 作者分析TIM下RL不稳定的根源，指出其主要由"漂移"（drift）导致，即训练与推理引擎之间随每步训练持续累积的持久性偏差。基于此推导出一个可加的"分数中心化"（score centering）修正项，通过抵消漂移来稳定RL训练。该修正项为可加形式，因此可与重要性采样（importance sampling）方法组合使用。

Result: 在0.6B至30B参数规模模型上训练时，仅使用分数中心化即可匹配甚至超越量化条件下的重要性采样方法，且不匹配越严重时优势越大。由于其可加特性，分数中心化还能与重要性采样组合，在陈旧性（staleness）实验中组合方法优于纯重要性采样基线。

Conclusion: TIM下RL的不稳定性主要由训练与推理引擎间累积的漂移偏差造成；通过引入可加的分数中心化修正项能够有效抵消漂移、稳定训练，并在多种模型规模与更严重不匹配情形下达到或超过重要性采样方法，且可与重要性采样叠加进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models is notoriously sensitive to small differences between training and inference engines, often referred to as the training-inference mismatch (TIM). However, completely eliminating TIM is impractical, as it would come at a major cost to rollout efficiency. In this paper, we show that the instability of RL under TIM is primarily caused by drift: a persistent bias between training and inference engines that accumulates with every training step. We derive an additive "score centering" correction term that stabilizes RL under TIM by canceling drift. When training models from 0.6B to 30B parameters, score centering alone matches or outperforms methods based on importance sampling under quantization, with the gap growing as the mismatch becomes more severe. Because the correction is additive, score centering also composes with importance sampling -- their composition outperforms pure importance-sampling baselines in our staleness experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Message capacity and claim wording set the transition points of collective truth-finding in language-model networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19183)
*Makoto Fukushima*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM群体在有限阅读量下形成错误共识的机制，发现结果主要由两个单智能体测量决定：主张措辞设定的阈值与消息容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在人类或LLM讨论中，智能体因认知、上下文或成本限制只能读取少量他人贡献，这种阅读上限在多大程度上单独决定了群体结局——即LLM群体即使在多数初始正确时也可能陷入错误共识。

Method: 用单个数（消息容量）建模阅读上限，确定智能体读取多少他人消息，并据此生成通信网络。通过31,824次随机查询，将8B参数模型对某主张的判断归结为收件箱加权和的logistic函数（等价于带除法归一化权重的随机二元神经元更新规则），并仅依据权重与网络度统计进行预测；另在1,414个指定起始的回合中检验，并在第二个8B模型与70B模型上重复与校准。

Result: 理论上，当智能体平均读取少于31个来源中的约6.4个时，错误共识应从任何起点都不可达。但1,414个回合的预测失败：从每个起点出发，正确方获胜比例低于50%，即使75%智能体初始正确时也仅28-45%。失败源于"场"（主张措辞在读取任何消息前就为回答设定的阈值），实验主张的场低于校准均值；使用各主张自身的场后，相同权重可复现结果。反转措辞表明阈值取决于主张所断言的内容而非其真假。第二个8B模型上该流程预测出主张依赖的双稳态，转变点出现在计算位置，八主张校准在16种条件中匹配15种。在70B模型上未检测到断言偏差。

Conclusion: 群体的命运在很大程度上由两个单智能体测量决定：主张措辞设定的阈值，以及决定转变点的消息容量。

Abstract: Whether human or large language model (LLM), an agent in a discussion reads only a few of the others' contributions, bounded by cognition, context, or cost. LLM collectives can settle on a wrong consensus even when a majority starts out correct; we ask how far that reading bound alone decides the outcome. We model the bound with one number, the message capacity, which sets how many of the others' messages an agent reads, and generate the communication network from it. Over 31,824 randomized queries, we found that an 8-billion-parameter model's judgment of a claim effectively reduces to a logistic function of a weighted sum of its inbox, the update rule of a stochastic binary neuron with divisively normalized weights. From these weights and the network's degree statistics alone, the wrong consensus should become unreachable from any start once agents read, on average, fewer than 6.4 of their 31 sources. In 1,414 episodes with assigned starts the prediction failed: the correct side won in fewer than 50% of episodes from every start, and in only 28-45% when 75% of agents started correct. The failure traces to the field, the threshold that a claim's wording sets for the agent's answer before any message is read: the experimental claims' fields lay below the calibration mean, and with each claim's own field the same weights reproduce the outcomes. Reversing the wording showed that the threshold follows what a claim asserts, not whether it is true. On a second 8B model the pipeline predicts claim-dependent bistability; transition points appeared where computed, and an eight-claim calibration matched in 15 of 16 conditions. At 70B the assertion bias is not detected. Thus a collective's fate is largely set by two single-agent measurements: the threshold a claim's wording sets, and the message capacity that sets the transition point.

</details>


### [282] [CC-OPI: Online Distributed Task Allocation for UAV Swarms under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19208)
*Biao Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出CC-OPI算法，通过事件驱动的方式将任务协商与物理执行交织，解决通信受限下多无人机集群任务分配的间歇连接问题，任务完成率约0.80。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人任务（如灾后搜救）中，短通信范围将无人机集群割裂为临时信息孤岛。在间歇连接下，主流的"先分配后执行"范式（要求任何物理移动前先达成全局共识）会失效，因此需要一种能在通信受限环境下在线执行任务分配的新方法。

Method: 提出CC-OPI（通信受限在线性能影响）算法，一种事件驱动方法，将任务协商与物理执行交织。仅在离散的物理与拓扑事件时重规划。包含两方面设计：一是适配动态拓扑的代价评估指标——空间局部性惩罚（促进区域化作业）与截止时间感知的紧迫性项，并辅以非抢占式状态锁保护每个无人机的进行中动作；二是去中心化容错层，结合基于版本的状态同步与全局时间驱动的应急池。

Result: 理论上证明CC-OPI在有限时间内终止，无过期完成死锁，且在任务时域内无无限重分配。在250米通信半径的仿真中，CC-OPI任务完成率约0.80：领先未修改PI与CBBA规则的匹配在线执行约7个百分点，超过朴素迁移的静态基线约20个百分点，在完全连接下与PI和CBBA相差仅几个百分点。在测试设置中，CC-OPI随连接性减弱而优雅退化，并能吸收丢包、地形遮挡和运行时任务到达。

Conclusion: CC-OPI有效应对通信受限下的多无人机任务分配，代价是更多消息开销和一定冗余行程，这是以效率换取鲁棒性的有意权衡。该工作属于Other类别，不涉及LLM或MLLM。

Abstract: In multi-robot missions such as post-disaster search and rescue, a short communication range fragments a swarm of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) into transient information islands. Under such intermittent connectivity, the prevailing "allocate-then-execute" paradigm--which requires global consensus before any physical movement--breaks down. This paper proposes the Communication-Constrained Online Performance Impact (CC-OPI) algorithm, an event-driven method that interleaves task negotiation with physical execution. CC-OPI replans only at discrete physical and topological events and integrates two further elements. The first is a pair of cost-evaluation metrics adapted to dynamic topologies--one with a spatial locality penalty that promotes regionalized operation, the other with a deadline-aware urgency term--complemented by a non-preemptive state lock that shields each UAV's ongoing action. The second is a decentralized fault-tolerance layer that pairs version-based state synchronization with a global-time-driven emergency pool. We establish that CC-OPI terminates in finite time, free of stale-completion deadlock and of unbounded reassignment within the mission horizon.
